MINISTRY OF COMMERCE 
ORDER 

New Delhi, the 25th June, 1977 

8.0. 2171.-— Whereas the Central Government is of opinion 
that it is necessary and expedient so to do for the development 
of the export trade of India that in exercise of the powers 
conferred by section 6 of the Export (Quality Control and 
Inspection) Act, 1963 (22 of 1963) INDUSTRIAL CHAINS 
should be subject to quality control and inspection prior to 
export; 

And whereas the Central Government has formulated the 
proposals specified below for the said purpose and has for- 


warded the same to the Export Inspection Council as required 
by sub-rule (2) of rule 11 of the Export (Quality Control 
and Inspection) Rules, 1964; 

Now, therefore, in pursuance of the said sub-rule the Cen- 
tral Government hereby published the said proposals for the 
information of the public likely to be affected thereby. 

2, Notice is hereby given that any person desiring to for- 
ward any objections or suggestions with respect to the said 
proposals may forward the same within forty-five days of the 
date of publication of this order in the Gazette of India to 
the Export Inspection Council, "World Trade Centre”, 14/lB, 
Ezra Street (7th floor), Calcutla-70()001 . 



■flpCT'Ff Tnrra . 25, 1977/3niT 4, 1SB9 


['(mrll — 'W 3{ii)] 

PKOPOSAI s 

(IJ To nolily ih.it INDUSTRIAL CHAINS shall be ^ubJcet 
lo quality coriliol and inspection prioi to export; 

(2) To specify the type of inspection in accoixlancc with 
the draft Lvpoti ol INDUSTRIAL CHAINS (Quality Con- 
trol and Inspection) Rules, 1976 set out in Annexure — I to 
this C>ider as the type of Qutility contiol and inspection which 
would be .applied lo such INDUSTRIAL CHAINS prior to 
export ; 

(3) To lecognise — 

(a) the specifications as stipul.iied in the Hxpoit contiact 

between the foreisii buyer and the exporter; 

(b) In the .ibsence of^imy stipulation icfeired to in clause 

(a) the specificahons issued by the Indian Standards 
Institution or National Standards of a foreign conn- 
liy for INDUSTRIAL CHAINS as the standard 
specifications. 

4, To prohibit the export in the course of international 
trade of any such INDUSTRlAl CHAINS unless the same 
arc accompanied by a certificate issued by any one of the 
agencies established by the Central Government under section 
7 of the Fxport (Quality Control .md Inspection) Act, 1963 (22 
of 1963), the effect that the INDUSTRIAL CH.MNS satisfy 
the conditions relating lo quality control and inspection and 
arc export-worthy. 

3. Nothing in (his order shall .ipply lo the export by land, 
sea or air of boiiaflde samples of INDUSTRIAL CILMNS to 
piospective buyci's. 

4. In this Order — “Industrial Thain’’ shall mean any steel 
roller-chain, bush chain or leaf chain of simple or multiple 
cxinstruction used for transmission of power or for mechanical 
handling in .iny indusirial and other allied field. 

ANNb\URE-I 

Diafl 1 Liles proposed to be made under section 17 of the 
Fxport ((Quality Control and Inspection) Act, 1963, 

1. Short tide and commencement — (1) These rules may be 
called the Fxport of 1NDU.STRIAL CHAINS ((Iluality Con- 
trol and Inspection). Rules, 1977. 

2. Dcfini;ions.--In these rules, unless the context otherwise 
leqiiires — 

(. 1 ) “Act” means ihe Export (Quality Control and Inspec-

ORDER 


S.O. 2172 . — ^Whereas, in exei-cise of the power.s conferred 
by section 6 of the Export (Quality Control and Inspection) 
Act, 196.1 (22of 1963), the Central Ciovemmcnt is of opi- 
nion that it is necessary and expedient so to do for the deve- 
lopment of the export trade of India that Beche-de-mcr should 
be subject to quality control and inspection prior to export ; 


And whereas the Central Government has fomuihited the 
proposals .specified lielow for the said purpose and has for- 
warded the same to the Export Inspection Council, as re- 
quired by sub-rule (2) of rule 11 of the Export 1 Quality 
Control and Inspecion), Rules, 1964 ; 


Now, therefore, in pursuance of ibe said snb-rule, (he 
Central Government hereby publishes the said proposals for 
the information of the public likely to be affected thereby. 

Notice is hereby given that any person desiring to forwnid 
any objections or suggestions with respect to the said pro- 
posals may foiward the .same within -15 days of the dale of 
publication of this order in the Ofiici d Gazette to the Ex- 
port Inspection Council of India, 14/IB, Ezui Stie^i (7lh 
floor), Calcutta 700001.

New Delhi, Ihc 13th June, 1977 

S.O. 2173, — WhcrCHs h public nolice I'or levising the local 
aica o£ Durg Bhilai Telephone Exchange System ev.cs publi- 
shed as required by rule 434 (III) (2c) of the Indian Tele- 
graph Rules, 1951 in the Newspapers m circulation at Durg 
Bhilai, inviting objections and suggestions from all peisons 
likely to be affected thereby, within a period of 30 days from 
the date of publication of the notice in the Newspapers ; 

And v-hereas the said notice was made available to the 
public on 2Kth March, 1977 in newspapers M.P. Chionicle’ 
‘Nav Bharat’ and ‘Hitvada’; 

And whereas no objections and suggestions have been 
received from the public on the said notice ; 

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferied by 
iiile 434 (TII) (2c) of the said Rules, the Directoi ticneuil 
Posts and Telegraphs hereby declares that with effcei fiom 
1-7-77 the revised local area of Durg-Bhilai shall be as 
under : 


Diirg-Bhllal Telephone Exchange System 

The local area of Durg-Bhilai shall cover an area falling 
under the jurisdiction of Special Area Development .Authority 
Hhilai-Durg presently covering Durg-Bhilai and Khiiisipnr 
hxcHftnges as declared vide notification No. 2C4yb-ll-6 
XXXIII /74 dated 23-1-74, published In M P. Ciiizette dated 
15-3-1974 on page No, 207 ; 

Provided that the telephone subscribers located oiUsidc the 
area under special Area Development Authority dcsciibed 
above shall continue to pay local tariffs as long as they are 
located wilhin 5 Kms of any exchange of this system and 
remain connected to it.
